  in CI; this scenario is the check that the names are RIGHT, which no test can assert.

S90 | QA module — sync, queue, playback, timestamped comments | Subsystem: Server, Client (QA views)
  Steps:
    - Operator prep: set `QA_SS_ID` (fresh dedicated spreadsheet), `QA_RECORDINGS_FOLDER_ID`
      (a Drive folder the deploying account can read), and `QA_MEMBERS` (the QA rep's email —
      by hand, or since operator note 8 (2026-09-10) in Manage → Admin → Config → **QA reviewers**)
    - Drop two audio files + one PDF into the folder; as the QA rep, open QA → Recordings →
      **Sync from Drive** → the toast reports 2 indexed · 1 non-audio skipped; re-run Sync →
      0 new (idempotent)
    - Confirm the queue cards show status/assignee pills + comment counts; Claim one card
      (pill flips to "you"); as a NON-manager try to Claim a card assigned to someone else →
      refused; as a manager, Assign… accepts a `QA_MEMBERS` entry OR another MANAGER
      (`qaCanReviewEmail_` — before note 8 the very managers `canSeeQa_` admits were
      refused) and names the Admin card for anyone else
    - Open a recording → "Loading audio — part N of M…" then the player renders; space
      plays/pauses, ←/→ seek 5s, the speed chips change playbackRate (aria-pressed follows)
    - Play to a moment, type a note, press "Comment at m:ss" → the comment appears in the
      list AND as a timeline marker; click the marker or the time chip → playback jumps there
    - Remove your own comment (author) and, as a manager, someone else's (moderation);
      confirm the QaComments row flips to `deleted` rather than disappearing
    - Drop a >40 MB recording, Sync, open it → the player area explains the cap and offers
      "Open in Drive ↗" instead
    - From an AGENT's login, confirm the QA tool appears NOWHERE (sidebar, mobile nav,
      `?tool=qaQueue` bounces); from the console call `google.script.run...getQaQueue()` →
      "QA access required."
    - **(Phase 2)** In the detail, confirm the Start review / Mark done / Reopen buttons
      actually change the status pill (the Phase-1 dead-handler fix); type an agent name
      into the **Agent** field (roster names suggest; free text works) → Save; open the
      **Scorecard** card → rate a few criteria 1–5 (clicking the selected number
      unselects it), add notes, Save → your card appears below; re-score and confirm the
      list shows ONE card of yours (latest wins); where the browser can decode the audio
      a waveform renders and clicking it seeks — an undecodable format keeps the plain
      timeline with nothing broken
    - **(Phase 2)** Open QA → **Stats** → the per-agent table shows recordings /
      reviewed / scorecards / averages with your attribution; "(unassigned)" counts the
      recordings nobody attributed; an agent with no scorecards reads em dashes, not 0
    - **(Phase 3)** On a scored recording with NO agent set, click **Share with
      agent** → refused, naming attribution as the missing step; set the Agent to a
      roster rep's exact name, Share → the "shared with agent" pill appears; as THAT
      rep's login (a QA member while the tool is hidden from agents — the
      2026-08-28 gate), open QA → **My Reviews** → the recording lists the
      scorecard + timestamped comments read-only, and (follow-on 2026-08-28) a
      **Play recording** button streams the audio through the SCOPED endpoint —
      confirm playback works, and that a DIFFERENT rep's My Reviews stays empty
      (name scope) and a direct `getMyQaReviewAudioChunk` call for the same id
      reads "Recording not found."; Unshare → the review leaves the agent's tab
      AND its playback refuses on next load
    - **(2026-08-28 gate)** As a non-QA rep, confirm the QA tool appears NOWHERE
      (sidebar, mobile nav; `?tool=qaMyReviews` bounces) — Phase 3 briefly showed
      every rep a My Reviews tab and the operator hid it again; as a QA member,
      confirm all FOUR tabs (Recordings / Stats / My Reviews / Log) render
    - **(2026-08-28 follow-on)** Manage → Admin → Config → **QA scorecard
      criteria**: relabel a criterion → Save → the scorecard form + Stats columns
      pick it up; add a criterion and remove it; save the app-default list
      exactly → Script Properties shows NO `QA_SCORECARD_CRITERIA` entry; a bad
      key ("BAD KEY") is rejected by name with nothing written
    - **(Phase 3)** Click **"Sample 3 for me"** on the queue → up to 3 new+unassigned
      recordings flip to "you", drawn from the least-reviewed agents; on Stats,
      score one recording as TWO different reviewers and confirm a **Calibration**
      row appears (reviewer averages, spread, widest criterion gap) — a recording
      scored by one reviewer never appears there
    - **(operator note 8, 2026-09-10)** Manage → Admin → Config → **QA reviewers**: add
      an address (Enter adds; a bad shape is refused naming it; a duplicate is
      dropped), Save → Script Property `QA_MEMBERS` holds the lowercased list and
      the new reviewer sees the QA tool on next load; remove every chip and Save →
      a danger confirm ("Clear all QA reviewers?") and the empty state SAYS every
      manager still reviews; as a non-admin manager call
      `google.script.run...saveQaMembers([])` → "Admin access required."
    - In the QA spreadsheet's shared AuditLog rows (`QaSync`/`QaStatusChange`/`QaAssign`/
      `QaCommentAdd`/`QaAgentSet`/`QaScorecardSave`/`QaShare`/`QaSample` on the ADP
      sheet's AuditLog tab), confirm counts/ids ONLY — no file names, no comment
      text, no agent names, no ratings
  Expected: the gate runs before any store/Drive access (the endpoints reject even with
  QA_SS_ID unset); playback bytes only ever come from files INSIDE the configured folder
  (a valid Drive id outside it reads "Recording not found."); sync is idempotent and its
  truncation is reported, never silent; an UNSHARED review is invisible even to its own
  agent, and a shared one is invisible to every other agent. Pinned by QA-1..QA-14 +
  `test_qa_gates_rejectNonMember`; the player itself is manual-only (no harness can
  drive `<audio>`).

S91 | Team punches calendar (Manage → Manage Time) | Subsystem: Server, Client (Time Clock views)
  Steps:
    - As a manager, open Manage → Manage Time and scroll to the **Team Punches** card below Live Status
    - Confirm a month calendar in the Time/PTO visual vocabulary: each day cell carries a reps-punched badge, a `+N` off-count, PTO dots, a holiday star, and an amber tint when any rep's day is incomplete; today and the selected day read as TWO distinct states (selection is an `--info` inset ring)
    - Click a past weekday → the full-width table below shows one row per rep (ClockIn / LunchOut / LunchIn / ClockOut / Hours), ADJ chips on adjusted punches, warn-tinted incomplete rows, and ABSENT reps as muted "no punches" rows
    - Confirm a rep who is OFF that day appears once (as off), not twice — the absent merge dedupes by id AND name
    - Click a row's pencil → the Day Edit modal opens PREFILLED to that rep and that date; confirm no pencil renders on a row older than `ADJUST_WINDOW_DAYS`
    - Navigate ‹ back several months, then try to navigate PAST the current month
    - Re-enter the tab → the card paints instantly from the session cache and refreshes behind the pill; break the RPC (or go offline) mid-session → the painted month stays with a warn toast, and a COLD failure renders the warn error card instead
    - If Timesheet archiving is enabled, navigate to a month older than the archive window
    - As a non-manager, call `google.script.run...getTeamCalendar('2026-08')` from the console
  Expected: the non-manager call returns a bare `{error:'Manager access required.'}` (a READ gate — never `success:false`). Hours come from the same `calcHours_` arithmetic as payroll, so a day missing a clock-out reads INCOMPLETE, never 0; a garbage COMMENTS value is not a punch; a padded `" Approved "` PTO status still counts as off; offboarded rows are excluded from both the rows AND the roster count. The month is derived from the MANAGER's roster timezone, not browser-local. A month predating the live Timesheet tab says so (`archiveNote`) rather than presenting a short day as complete — the calendar is live-tab-only by design, and the export is the archive-aware path. **PTO2 (2026-09-16):** each off chip also carries the rep's remaining annual balance after a thin separator — this is where the approve decision is actually made. A rep the balance does not apply to (pto-disabled) carries NO balance segment rather than a zero, and a chip whose rep is missing from `liveStatus` renders exactly as before.

S92 | Approved punch adjustment catches up on the rep's screen | Subsystem: Server, Client (Time Clock views)
  Steps:
    - As a rep who forgot to clock in, open Adjust → request a same-day ClockIn → submit
    - WITHOUT reloading, look above the punch buttons: an info chip reads "Awaiting approval · Clock In 08:30" and says the manager still has to approve it
    - As a manager, approve the request from the dashboard queue
    - Back on the rep's Dashboard — still not reloaded, tab left VISIBLE — wait up to ~3 minutes
    - Repeat with a DENIAL on a second request
    - Navigate the rep to Call Notes and leave it there for several minutes, watching the Network tab
    - Background the browser tab entirely for several minutes, then return
  Expected: the punch buttons catch up on their own — Clock In is replaced by Lunch Out / Clock Out without a reload, because an approved `ADJ-ClockIn` is a real punch and `getNextActions_` has always said so; the pending chip disappears with it. BOTH outcomes email the rep (branded, with an Open Time Clock button); the denial says explicitly that no change was made to the timesheet, so a denial is never mistaken for an approval that hasn't propagated. The reconcile rides the EXISTING 1Hz tick (no second interval) and is gated to the Clock view being open AND visible — Call Notes and a backgrounded tab make ZERO reconcile calls, and returning to a visible Clock view refreshes without queueing a burst. A rep with no pending adjustments sees no chip at all.

S93 | Response times count business hours | Subsystem: Server, Client (Metrics views), Client (Time Clock views)
  Steps:
    - Have a Spanish request arrive Friday afternoon and be answered Monday morning (or hand-set the stamps)
    - As a manager or bilingual member, open Metrics → Spanish Inbox
    - Read the Avg time and Median cells, their sub-lines, and the note beneath the strip
    - Hover a resolved card's green duration
    - Open the Dashboard and read the Spanish card's "median reply"
    - Open Metrics → Dept Requests and walk the business-hours step in S74
    - Check the daily SLA reminder email against what the tracker shows
    - Set a request's stamps so the pair is unusable (reverse them, or blank one)
    - **(operator notes 2/3, 2026-09-10)** Mark one Spanish request resolved MANUALLY and resolve one Dept Request in the APP; read both strips again after the 5-min Spanish cache rolls
  Expected: the KPI cells lead with the BUSINESS figure and carry "wall clock <X>" as their sub-line, under a note naming the window ("Response times count 8 AM–5 PM business hours only — weekends and US holidays excluded"); the Friday→Monday case reads as ~2 hours, not ~3 days. The resolved card's tooltip shows both units. The Dashboard median matches the tab (same unit, not two numbers for one thing). An unusable pair is DROPPED from the sample and renders as unknown — never 0, which would drag the median down. A span falling entirely outside business hours legitimately reads 0: that is "nothing was owed during it", which is different from unknown. Deploy skew is safe in both directions: an older client ignores the additive fields, and an older server makes the client fall back to wall clock as the headline WITH the note suppressed (it must never claim an exclusion it didn't make). **(operator notes 2/3) A manual Spanish resolve and an in-app Dept Requests resolve are EXCLUDED from the sample on their surface and NAMED beside the timed count** — the Spanish strip reads "· N timed · 1 marked manually, not timed" and the manually-resolved card's chip reads "not timed" (its title says why), the Dept Requests median sub-line names the excluded in-app resolves, and both figures DROP accordingly (the Spanish key is `spanish_inbox_v2`, so a pre-deploy cached median never serves); a resolve stamp records when someone pressed a button, not when the requester was answered, so it is never a duration.

S94 | Done-state names its evidence; the update prompt actually reloads | Subsystem: Client (Time Clock views), Client (shell)

S96 | Resuming a closed day, and the requests that reach a manager | Subsystem: Server, Client (Time Clock views)
  Steps:
    - As a rep, clock in and clock out so the Dashboard reads "Shift complete for today"
    - Press **Adjust** and look at the Punch Type field
    - Close it, press **Resume shift**, and read the confirmation before accepting
    - Accept, then look at the punch area without reloading
    - Press Resume shift again (if it is still there)
    - As a MANAGER, check your inbox, then open Manage → Manage Time
    - Read the pending row, approve it, and open the rep's day in Day Edit
    - As the rep, check your inbox and your punch buttons
    - Separately: file an ordinary missed-punch adjustment and check the manager inbox
    - Edge case: as a manager, delete the rep's clock-out via Day Edit while a resume request is still pending, then approve it
  Expected: Adjust opens with **Clock In** already selected — the punch that state implies is missing. The Resume confirmation states the consequence BEFORE you accept: your clock-out becomes a break ending now, so the time you were away is **unpaid**, and nothing changes until a manager approves. After accepting, the chip above the punch buttons reads "Resume shift · back at HH:MM" — never "Clock Out HH:MM", which is the punch it converts, not one it adds — and the Resume button disappears (a second press is refused server-side anyway). The manager is EMAILED — both for a resume and for an ordinary adjustment; before this round that queue notified nobody. The queue row says what approving does ("clock-out becomes a break — the gap is unpaid"), not just a punch type. After approval the rep's day shows a break where the clock-out was, at the SAME time, closed at the resume time — Day Edit lists it as a break pair — and the day is open again, so the punch buttons offer Clock Out. The decision email says the same thing. **The edge case must REFUSE**: with the clock-out deleted, approving reports that there is no clock-out any more and tells the manager to deny — the request must NOT be marked Approved, because converting a punch that is gone would leave an unpaired break half that the hours arithmetic silently drops. A genuine second SHIFT (two separate stints on one date) is still not supported — use Day Edit.

S97 | Admin → System is findings-first and reaches all-clear | Subsystem: Client (Call Notes views)
  Steps:
    - As an admin, open Manage → Admin; read the three Overview status cards, then click one
    - On the System tab, read the "Needs attention" list, then open the "N checks passing" disclosure
    - In the Storage inventory table, press a row's chevron
    - Break `CDR_SS_ID` (or use `?failrpc=getAutomationHealth` on the visual harness) and reload the Admin tab
    - On a healthy deployment with dozens of off-roster CDR agents and HR/QA stores left unset, read the System tab
    - Read the THREE capability lines ABOVE the inventory table, in order (Drive, mail routing, Script Properties); then set `MAIL_BCC_ALL` to an internal address and reload, and again to an address on another domain
    - Narrow to a phone width and open the System tab
  Expected: each Overview card is a BUTTON that lands on the System tab scrolled to its own section (Automation / CDR / Storage), and its aria-label names the destination. The System tab's badge equals the number of items in "Needs attention"; every item carries a Blocking/Warning pill, a title, the detail, a fix line, and — where the server supplied one — an open link. Blocking items sort first. The chevron expands the store's detail row (note / per-rep problems / the exact tz fix naming the CONFIG zone) with `aria-expanded` following. With the health read broken, the Automation and CDR cards read **Unavailable**, the findings list carries a Blocking "Automation health could not be read" item, and the Automation detail slot renders the warn card — never "All OK". On the healthy deployment the list reads **Nothing needs attention** and the off-roster agents, the no-CDR roster names, and the unset no-fallback stores appear ONLY inside "checks passing" with their counts (INV-186 — a tab that can never go green trains the reader to ignore it). **THREE capability lines sit above the table — Drive, then mail routing, then Script Properties** — none is a spreadsheet, so no store row can carry them (F3, cycle 19; Script Properties added in Batch Q). The Script Properties line states bytes used of the 500KB store and the largest single value against the ~9KB per-value cap; on a comfortable store it is an `ok` FACT inside "checks passing" and the badge count does NOT rise, it warns only past 80% of either cap, and an unreadable read renders as unknown rather than OK. Values are COUNTED, never echoed into the panel. With `MAIL_BCC_ALL` unset the mail line is an `ok` FACT inside "checks passing", so the tab still reaches Nothing-needs-attention; with an internal address it is a standing WARNING naming the address and stating that those copies carry intake bodies (full patient answers) and department emails (patient name + TRX); with an off-domain address it is BLOCKING and sorts to the top. Reporting never changes what is sent — an address the operator typed is always honoured. At 390px nothing scrolls sideways; the finding cards stack their pill above the title.

S99 | Coaching surface — signal board, drawer, business-day overdue, reply, critical-only mail, weekly recap | Subsystem: Server, Client (Training views)
  Steps:
    - As a manager, open Training → Coaching (Team); read the KPI strip and the note beneath it, then the "Who needs a 1-on-1" board
    - Press **Log coaching** → the drawer slides in from the right; switch Coaching ⇄ Praise and watch the severity chips + coaching point hide; press Escape
    - From Call Notes → Team Notes → Per-Rep, press **Coach on this** on a note → the drawer opens prefilled (employee, TRX, narrative, a linked-note chip)
    - Log a MINOR item, then a CRITICAL item with a revisit date, for a rep whose roster row carries a login email; check that rep's inbox and yours
    - Void the critical item with a reason; check the rep's inbox again; open the Voided filter
    - Press **Nudge** on an open item twice in one day
    - As the rep, open Coaching; read the callout, press Jump to it, type a reply, Acknowledge; as the manager, check your inbox and the card
    - Log a praise item; as the rep, look for an Acknowledge button on it
    - Hand-set an item's `CreatedAt` to a Friday 16:00 and read its age on Monday morning
    - From the editor run `sendCoachingRecapDigest` as a manager; check an agent's inbox
    - Narrow to 390px on both the manager and the rep view
  Expected: the note reads "Ages, the overdue window and the median count business days only"; the board tiers each rep (a rep with any critical in 30 days is PRIORITY; one with nothing in 21 days reads the INFO-toned "No signal", not Clear). The drawer is a named dialog (Escape closes it through the shell). The prefilled drawer carries the note DATE, and the saved card's note chip drills back to that rep + date. **Only the critical item emails the rep at once** (cc you) — the mail names who logged it, when, the severity and the revisit date, and NOTHING of the narrative or TRX; the minor item sends nothing. Voiding the critical item sends a "Withdrawn" mail; the voided item appears ONLY under the Voided filter (All excludes it) and never in the rep's view. The second Nudge is refused ("once per day per item"). The rep's callout names the oldest open item's age in business days; after Acknowledge the card dims, the reply shows under "Your reply", and your inbox says they replied WITHOUT quoting it. The praise item carries NO Acknowledge button and sits in the Recognition feed on both views; it counts in neither "Awaiting ack" nor the ack rate. The Friday-16:00 item reads well under a day old on Monday. The recap lists the minor + praise items with severity/date/logged-by and no narrative; the critical item is absent from it. At 390px the KPI strips go 2×2, the board drops its Mix and Last columns, and nothing scrolls sideways.

S98 | Manage surface — grouped Manage Time, Punctuality diagnostics, Coverage presets | Subsystem: Server, Client (Time Clock views)
  Steps:
    - As a manager, open Manage → Manage Time; read the order of the cards and the two group headings
    - Press the Periodic heading's toggle; press it again; reload the page
    - Wait for the PTO reconciliation and sheet doctor cards to load (they are inside the collapsed group) and read the Periodic summary line WITHOUT expanding; break one of those reads (e.g. `?failrpc=getPtoReconciliation` on the harness) and read it again
    - Open Manage → Punctuality; click 7d / 30d / QTR, then Custom… and type a range of 100 days → Load
    - Read the summary strip (team on-time %, the "vs prior N days" delta, worst rep + date); confirm the Outliers panel appears only when a rep is under 75% or averages >15 min late
    - Press a rep's chevron → read the day strip (hover a day; tab to it with a screen reader running), the weekly trend chip, and press **Coach on this**
    - In the Coaching composer that opens, read the pre-filled employee and the "what" text; cancel
    - Temporarily rename the `TimeOffRequests` tab and reload Punctuality for a range where a rep was on approved PTO
    - Open Manage → Coverage; click This week / Next week / Next 2 weeks; leave the tab open across midnight (or hand-set the clock) and re-enter
    - Narrow all three tabs to 390px
  Expected: Manage Time reads Needs you (Pending Time Off warn-toned with "oldest N days", Missed Clock-Outs destructive-toned, adjustment queue, Live Status, Team Punches) THEN Periodic collapsed; the toggle's `aria-expanded` follows it; a reload re-collapses (session-only, never persisted); the summary line reads "PTO drift: all clear · Sheet doctor: all clear · N recent punches · M activities" when clean, names the drift when not, and reads "check failed" (warn) when a read fails — never "all clear". Punctuality's presets press like Metrics'; the 100-day range is refused by name ("at most 92 days"); the delta compares against the immediately preceding range of equal length; the day strip carries an `aria-label` that summarises it in words; the trend chip reads Worsening / Flat / Improving; Coach on this lands on the Coaching composer in Team mode with the rep selected and the narrative in the "what" field. With the PTO read broken the note names it and a rep's PTO day reads "no punch" rather than "late". Coverage's presets look FORWARD; a default range re-anchors at midnight while a manually chosen one does not. At 390px nothing scrolls sideways; the punctuality table drops Shift start / Avg late / Lunch and the detail stacks.

S95 | A day with more than one break is counted, displayed and repaired correctly | Subsystem: Server, Client (Time Clock views)
  Steps:
    - As a rep, punch a split day: Clock In 08:00, Lunch Out 12:00, Lunch In 12:30, Lunch Out 17:00, Lunch In 19:00, Clock Out 21:00 (the sequence is already legal — Lunch Out is re-offered after Lunch In)
    - Separately, hand-edit a day so it carries a STRAY EARLY Lunch In — outs 12:00, ins 11:00 + 12:30 on an 08:00–17:00 day — and read its hours
    - Open Time / PTO → the day's hours
    - Open the pay statement for that period and read the day row
    - As a manager, open Manage → Manage Time → Team Punches and select that date
    - Run the sheet doctor (the manager-dashboard card) and read what it offers
    - Repeat with a day carrying a STRAY Lunch Out and no return
    - As a manager in the editor, run `reportMultiBreakDays()` and read the execution log
    - **(2026-09-11)** Run `reportBreakPairingChanges()` the same way; then run the sheet doctor against the stray-early-in day and against a two-break day whose LEAVE was double-punched
  Expected: the day computes **10.5h**, not 11.0 — both break pairs are deducted, where before only the last was and the 12:00 lunch was silently paid. **The stray-early-in day computes 8.5h, not 9.0** — the 11:00 `in` is dropped ALONE and the real 12:00–12:30 break is still deducted; walking both lists on one index shifted every later `in` a slot and un-paired the whole day, paying the rep for every break they took (F1, cycle 19). The same figure appears on the timesheet, the pay statement, the team calendar and the PTO accrual, because all five read through `punchDayAdd_` + `calcHours_`. The scalar `lunchOut`/`lunchIn` still render the FIRST break, so every existing display is unchanged on an ordinary single-break day, and a rep who is on lunch RIGHT NOW (a Lunch Out with no return yet) still shows that stamp. The sheet doctor does NOT offer to collapse the matched pair — that would delete recorded unpaid time and start paying for it — but it still flags the stray unpaired Lunch Out, and still flags a repeated Clock In/Clock Out (multi-shift is not supported). `reportMultiBreakDays()` writes nothing and lists every historical rep/date that changes, with old vs new hours; every listed day gets SHORTER. Opening that day in **Day Edit** now shows a Breaks list with both pairs and a per-pair remove button; saving it unchanged writes nothing, and removing a pair deletes exactly that pair's rows (A4, same day — the modal's four fixed slots are gone). **(2026-09-11)** `reportBreakPairingChanges()` writes nothing and lists the stray-early-in day as 9.0h → 8.5h (delta −0.5) and NOTHING else — the ordinary split day pairs identically under both rules and must not appear. The doctor lists BOTH test days under "Unpaired break stamps", naming the dropped stamp (`return 11:00:00`; `leave HH:mm:ss` for the double-punched leave) beside the pairs it kept, offers NO collapse for either, and the Manage Time Periodic summary counts them ("… · N unpaired"); the classic one-lunch-type duplicate group is still offered for collapse exactly as before.
  Steps:
    - As a rep with a ClockOut already on today's rep-local date (an offshore rep pre-flip, or clock out on purpose in a test account), open the Dashboard
    - Read the punch area
    - Have a manager approve a same-day ClockIn adjustment for that rep, then reload the rep's page
    - Separately: cut a New-version deploy, leave a tab open ~20 min, and press **Reload** on the "Team Tools was updated" toast
    - Repeat that from a pinned COMPACT pop-out
  Expected: the message reads "Shift complete for today · clocked out at <time>" with a quiet "Didn't clock out? Use Adjust to add a missing punch, or ask your manager." below it — never a bare assertion, so a rep who has NOT worked yet can see which punch the app is reasoning from. Adjust remains the only action. After the approved adjustment, the trailing ClockIn wins the backward scan and **Lunch Out / Clock Out / Adjust** return (the `ADJ-` prefix is stripped on read, so an approved adjustment is a real punch — INV-09). The rep's own screen does NOT self-update on the currently-deployed code; they reload, or alt-tab away and back (`clkRefreshState_` on focus). The toast's Reload navigates the TOP window to the real `/exec` URL and the app comes back — **not** a white inner frame needing the browser's reload button. From a pop-out it comes back AS the pop-out, on the same tool.

S100 | QA surface — coverage-first queue, exemptions, pause-and-pin comments, coaching hand-off | Subsystem: Server, Client (QA views)
  Steps:
    - As a QA member, open QA → Recordings; read the period control, the four summary cards, and the coverage table; switch to the quarter and back
    - Reload the page → the last period you picked is still selected; in DevTools set `localStorage.umsQaPeriod` to `junk` and reload
    - Press **Sample the gaps for me (N)** → up to N recordings flip to you, drawn from the shortest-covered agents; with everyone at target the button is disabled
    - As a MANAGER, on a row reading Covered with two good periods, press **Grant exemption** → confirm → the row reads Exempt with target 0/0 and a Revoke button; as a non-manager QA member, confirm no Grant/Revoke buttons render and `google.script.run...qaSetExemption('x','2026-09',true)` returns "Manager access required."
    - Open a recording → read the header actions; press **Skip…** with a blank reason (refused), then with a reason → the row on the queue shows the reason under the Skipped pill; Reopen it
    - Press play, start typing a comment → playback PAUSES and the pin row reads the moment; seek elsewhere, keep typing → the pin does not move; edit the pin to `4:05` and press **Post & stay paused** → the comment lands at 4:05 and the player stays paused; type another and press **Post & resume**; type a third, set the pin to `9:99` and post → refused by name; Discard
    - Rate three criteria → the running average and "3 of 5 rated" update; save → your card's chips are toned (≤2 red, 3 neutral, ≥4 green)
    - Set the Agent to a roster name → the match line reads "On the roster · n of 3 sampled"; as a manager press **Coach on this call** → the Coaching composer opens with the rep selected and the timestamped comments as the narrative; set the Agent to a non-roster spelling → the match line warns and the coach button gives way to a note
    - Open the queue → the recording you just played shows a Length; temporarily rename the `QaScorecards` tab and reload the queue
    - As a shared agent (a QA member for now), open My Reviews → read the callout at the top, the per-card average, and the ±5s / speed transport after Play
    - Narrow to a phone width on the queue and the detail
  Expected: the period options come from the server (the client never computes them) and `junk` lands on the current month with no error; the summary strip agrees with the table beneath it (it is derived from the same rows — an over-sampled rep never covers for a short one, and the team average is call-weighted); the sampler never pulls a covered agent ahead of a short one and refuses BY NAME when everyone is at target; the exemption ledger's audit row carries the period and the flag, never the name; the skip reason never reaches the shared AuditLog either; a comment posts at the PIN, never at the live playhead; a typo'd pin is refused rather than posted at 0:00; with the scorecard tab unreadable the coverage block renders the warn card and the recordings table below is unaffected — never an empty coverage table; the detail is two panes from 1000px and a single column below with the player first; nothing scrolls sideways at 390px. Verified on camera: `qa-queue-{light,dark}-wide`, `qa-queue-light-mobile`, `qa-queue-empty-light-wide`, `qa-detail-light-{wide,mobile}`, `qa-stats-light-wide`, `qa-myreviews-light-wide`.

S101 | Time Clock surface — Needs you, actions-first rail, state line, held rotator, break-chip states | Subsystem: Server, Client (Time Clock views)
  Steps:
    - As a rep with an overdue coaching item, an unsigned doc, a call-back due today, a call without a note yesterday, pending training and an open dept request, open the Dashboard at a desktop width
    - Read what sits directly under the clock card, then the block at the top of the main column
    - Click the "calls without a note" row; go back; click the coaching row
    - Acknowledge the coaching item and return to the Dashboard (re-enter the tab)
    - Temporarily rename the `ScheduledCalls` tab (or use `?failrpc=getMyScheduledCalls`-equivalent by breaking that one read) and reload
    - On a deployment (or a test instance) where `HR_DOCS_SS_ID` is deliberately UNSET, reload and read the block's foot; then reload twice inside two minutes and watch the Network tab
    - Complete a listed item — mark the training read, acknowledge the coaching note, sign the doc, close the call-back, resolve the dept request — and return to the Dashboard INSIDE two minutes
    - Clear every item (or use `?fixture=empty` on the harness) and reload
    - Break `getMyPendingTasks` entirely (`?failrpc=getMyPendingTasks` on the harness)
    - Open the pinned COMPACT pop-out and watch the Network tab
    - Read the clock card's bottom row, the greeting sentence and the shift-strip header; clock out and read the card again
    - With a What's-new article configured, stay on the Dashboard while clocked in for 30s; clock out and wait again
    - Read the Breaks row at 10:50 (before B1), at 12:45 (during lunch) and at 14:30
    - Alt-tab away and back within the same minute and re-read the Breaks row
    - Narrow to 390px
  Expected: the punch buttons sit under the clock card (top ≈367px at 1440×900 — `node test/visual/fold-measure.mjs` prints it), with the shift strip BELOW them. The Needs-you block lists six rows overdue-first with the word "Overdue" on the overdue ones (a stronger tone when the due date has passed) and "N OVERDUE" in its head; the notes row lands on the Call Notes Log with the coverage-strip toast; the coaching row lands on Coaching. After the acknowledge, the re-entered Dashboard paints the last list instantly and refreshes it (the item drops on the refresh — never a loader over last-good). With one source broken the block still lists the other five and ends with "Couldn't check scheduled calls — those may have items too" — never a silent omission, and that round is NOT cached (reload within two minutes re-reads). **A store the operator never configured is DIFFERENT from a failed read (F2, cycle 19):** with `HR_DOCS_SS_ID` unset the block says NOTHING about coaching or employee docs — those features do not exist on that deployment and Storage Health is where an unset store is reported (INV-186) — the block can reach its clean-empty ABSENT state, and the second reload inside two minutes makes no server call, because a deliberately-unset store is not a reason to skip the cache. **Completing a listed item drops it immediately (F4):** the returning Dashboard no longer shows the finished item for the rest of the 120s TTL. With nothing outstanding the block is ABSENT (no "all clear" card). With the endpoint broken the block renders the warn error card, not an empty list. The compact pop-out makes NO `getMyPendingTasks` call and shows no block. The card's bottom row reads "On the clock · 5h 54m worked" on a dark scrim, the greeting reads "You're clocked in since 1:02 PM · 2h 33m until end of shift." with no hours, and the strip header carries only "% logged" + "Nm lunch" — the hours figure appears exactly once; after clock-out the row reads "Shift complete · 8h 00m worked". While clocked in the greeting NEVER rotates to a What's-new slide; after clock-out it does. The Breaks row shows B1 outlined "· in 10m" at 10:50, Lunch highlighted at 12:45, and B1 + Lunch struck through with B2 outlined "· in 30m" at 14:30; the states SURVIVE an alt-tab (the re-render must not ship plain chips). At 390px nothing scrolls sideways and the Needs-you rows stay two lines (title over detail). Verified on camera: `clock-light-wide`, `clock-dark-wide`, `clock-light-compact`, `clock-light-mobile`, `clock-pendingadj-light-wide`, `clock-needsyou-empty-light-wide`, `clock-needsyou-error-light-wide`. The Team Right Now card at the foot of the main column carries the presence chip (S14; operator note 10) — Leo Kim's `active · not clocked in` chip is on camera in all four Clock scenarios, and the head reads "Team Right Now" on ONE line with the summary beside it.

S102 | Break coverage planner — the strip on the Admin break-schedule card | Subsystem: Server, Client (Call Notes views)
  Steps:
    - As an admin, open Manage → Admin → Config → Break schedules; read the strip at the top before touching anything
    - Hover/click a cell in the lunch trough; read the line that appears; click it again
    - Customize Default and move Lunch from 12:30 to 13:30 WITHOUT saving; watch the strip
    - Add a per-agent section for one rep and stagger their lunch by 30 min; watch the strip; then leave a break row with a blank start time
    - Press Save; confirm the strip re-fills after the editor re-renders
    - Read the "Background bars" line; hover a cell and read its label
    - In the CDR Report spreadsheet, temporarily rename the `Inbound Calls` tab (or use `?failrpc=getBreakCoverage` on the harness for the cold path) and re-open the card
    - As a manager who is NOT an admin, call `google.script.run...getBreakCoverage({})` from the console
    - Narrow to 390px
  Expected: the strip shows one cell per quarter hour across the business window (8 AM–5 PM), each carrying the on-desk count and toned by the Coverage planner's thresholds (≥ good green, ≥ min amber, under min red), with the head line naming the agents on shift, the lowest desk and when, and how many per-agent schedules apply. Clicking a cell opens "12:30 PM–12:45 PM · 2 on desk of 8 on shift · away: <names> (Lunch)…" and marks the cell pressed; clicking again closes it. Moving Lunch re-derives the strip within a second WITHOUT saving (the copy says "Live preview"); the per-agent stagger moves only that rep; a half-typed row is simply skipped by the preview (Save still refuses it by name). After Save the strip repaints from the saved state. The background bars are average inbound calls per weekday per 15 min over the trailing 28 days, with the through-date and the four Customer Success queues named; the cell label carries the average. With the tab renamed the strip still renders and the note reads "Background call volume unavailable — the Inbound Calls tab is not in the CDR Report spreadsheet…"; with the whole RPC failing on a cold open the host renders the warn error card, never an empty strip, and a failed REFRESH after a good paint keeps the last strip with a warn line. The non-admin manager gets a bare `{error:'Admin access required.'}`. At 390px the strip scrolls inside its own box and the page never scrolls sideways. Verified on camera: `admin-config-light-wide`, `admin-config-light-mobile`, `admin-config-covfail-light-wide`.

S103 | QA Log — one entry per recording audited, typed rubric, the manual fallback | Subsystem: Server, Client (QA views), Client (Call Notes views)
  Steps:
    - As an admin, open Manage → Admin → Config → QA scorecard criteria; set one criterion's Type to **Yes / No** and add a new one typed **Dropdown** with options `Resolved, Escalated, Callback scheduled`; try `Yes, 3` as options; Save
    - As a QA member, open a recording's detail and read the scorecard form; answer the Yes/No pair, click the SAME answer again, pick a dropdown option, then the blank option; rate one scale criterion; Save
    - Open QA → **Log**; read the summary strip, the day groups, and the card you just saved; type part of the agent's name into the search box; click 7d / 30d / QTR, then Custom… with a 100-day range
    - As a MANAGER, pick a reviewer in the picker, then "All reviewers"; as a non-manager QA member, call `google.script.run...getQaLog({reviewer:'*'})` from the console and read `reviewer` in the response
    - Click an entry's title; go back to the Log and click **Log an audit** — pick one of your open assignments; then again and choose **No recording attached (manual entry)**, give an agent + label, Continue
    - On the manual recording's detail, read the player slot, save a scorecard, and re-open the Log; open QA → Stats and My Reviews (share it first) and find the same entry
    - Delete a scored recording's row from the `QaRecordings` tab by hand and reload the Log
    - Temporarily rename the `QaScorecards` tab and reload the Log; use `?fixture=empty` / `?failrpc=getQaLog` on the harness for the empty and error states
    - As a non-QA rep, confirm the Log tab appears nowhere and `getQaLog({})` returns "QA access required."
  Expected: the `Yes, 3` options are refused BY NAME ("a bare number — dropdown options must be words"), nothing written; the saved property carries `type`/`options` only on the typed criteria. On the form a check renders a Yes/No pair (clicking the pressed one UNSELECTS), a choice a NAMED select whose blank option clears it; the running average counts only the scale answer while "N of M rated" counts every answered criterion; the saved card's chips read "Verified two identifiers Yes" (accent) / "No" (destructive) and the option text untoned. The Log shows one entry per recording (re-scoring never adds a second), the strip and day groups DERIVE from the entries (an em dash where no scale answer exists — never 0), the search filters without a network call, and the 100-day range is refused as "at most 92 days". A non-manager's `reviewer` echoes THEIR OWN id whatever they asked (the client renders "Your audits"); a manager's picker changes the list. Clicking a title opens the queue detail on that recording (the hint is consumed — a later plain navigation never re-opens it). The manual entry opens with "This audit was logged without a recording attached" in the player slot and NO audio request; its row is pilled "no recording" on the Log, the queue, Stats (counted under its agent) and My Reviews (the pill in place of Play); its audit row carries only `fileId=manual-…`. The hand-deleted recording's audit STILL LISTS as "(recording no longer indexed)" with its ratings and notes, not clickable. With the scorecards tab unreadable the Log renders the warn error card, never an empty ledger; the empty twin reads "No audits logged between … Press Log an audit"; the error scenario renders `errorStateHtml_`. Verified on camera: `qa-log-light-wide`, `qa-log-light-mobile`, `qa-log-empty-light-wide`, `qa-log-error-light-wide`, `qa-log-new-light-wide`.

S104 | Drive capability — the Admin signal and the folder error that names itself | Subsystem: Server, Client (Call Notes views), Client (Reference views)
  Steps:
    - As an admin, open Manage → Admin → System and read the line above the Storage inventory table
    - Convert a Google Doc containing an image in Reference and press Save
    - With `KB_IMAGES_FOLDER_ID` UNSET and the Drive scope missing, read the save toast (it is STICKY — image warnings are, deliberately) and then the `KbItemSave` audit row's `imageWarnings=`
    - Set `KB_IMAGES_FOLDER_ID` to a folder the deploying account cannot open, and save again
    - Grant the scope (editor → run any function → accept), then re-open Admin → System
    - Break the probe (block the tokeninfo host, or run with an older server that ships no `drive` field)
    - Open Manage → Admin → Overview and read the Storage card + the System tab badge
  Expected: the line reads **Drive access granted · the KB Images folder is created on the first image export** on a healthy deployment (an unprovisioned folder is a FACT, not a warning — INV-186), and the same state rides "N checks passing". Without the scope it is a **BLOCKING** finding at the top of Needs attention, naming the scope, the three surfaces that break (article images, the embed check, QA playback) and the fix — re-authorize as the DEPLOYING account, because a clasp push never re-prompts. The save toast then says `KB Images folder: KB_IMAGES_FOLDER_ID is not set, and the folder could not be created: <Apps Script's own message> — the DEPLOYING account must re-authorize…`; with a dead stored folder it instead names the id, the OPEN reason, and that creating a replacement also failed — the half that used to vanish entirely. A Drive-SIDE failure (a quota) carries NO re-auth hint. A broken probe reads **could not be checked · treat as unknown, not OK** (warn), never a green line; an older server with no `drive` field renders NOTHING (deploy skew is safe in both directions). Opening the Admin tab must never CREATE a folder or a file — the probe introspects the token and reads the stored id only. Verified on camera: `admin-system-light-wide`, `admin-system-nodrive-light-{wide,mobile}`.

S105 | Spanish Inbox — manager auto-assign of unclaimed requests | Subsystem: Server, Client (Metrics views)
  Steps:
    - Ensure `SPANISH_INBOX_MEMBERS` lists ≥2 members and the window holds ≥3 pending requests (one already claimed, one a voicemail card **whose 8x8 `Duration:` is AT OR ABOVE `SPANISH_VM_MIN_SECONDS` (default 5s) — since 2026-09-16 a shorter one is suppressed from the pending list and is therefore not auto-assignable, so a 1-second fixture would silently stop covering the voicemail case**)
    - As a listed MEMBER who is not a manager, open Metrics → Spanish Inbox and look beside the filter strip
    - As a MANAGER, open the same tab; read the button's label; hover it while the list is still loading, and again with every request claimed
    - Press it → read the confirm; Cancel; press again → Confirm; watch the Network tab and the cards
    - Clear `SPANISH_INBOX_MEMBERS` (save an empty list from the Admin card) and reload
    - As a member, call `google.script.run...autoAssignSpanishThreads(7)` from the console
    - Inspect the `SpanishClaims` tab and the AuditLog
    - Narrow to 390px
    - **(2026-09-11, the scheduled twin)** Re-run `installAutomationTriggers()`; open Manage → Admin → System within the hour and read the "Spanish Inbox auto-assign" heartbeat row; flip the `spanishAutoAssign` toggle ON, leave requests unclaimed through one business-hours trigger firing, then read the cards; flip it OFF, leave a request unclaimed overnight, and read again; clear `SPANISH_INBOX_MEMBERS` with the toggle ON and read the health dot after the next firing
  Expected: the member sees NO button. The manager's button reads "Auto-assign N unclaimed" where N counts the UNCLAIMED pending requests only (the already-claimed card is not counted; the voicemail IS); it is disabled WITH THE REASON in its title while the list loads, when no members are configured, and when everything is already claimed. Cancelling the confirm makes NO request; confirming makes ONE `autoAssignSpanishThreads` call carrying the current days window, after which every pending card carries a claim pill — the requests went oldest-first to the member with the fewest live claims, alphabetical tie-break (a run over the same state picks the same members), and a claim someone else made between the read and the write is respected, never overwritten — the button reads 0 and says why, and ONE toast reports the count. The member's console call returns `Manager access required.` (the writer shape — this endpoint is deliberately outside the seven-endpoint `canSeeSpanishInbox_` tier). `SpanishClaims` gains one `claim` row per assigned request in the shape `spanishClaimsFold_` reads (`assignedBy` = the manager); the AuditLog's `SpanishInboxAutoAssign` row carries `assigned=N; members=M` only — no thread ids, no requester. At 390px the button wraps under the filter strip and nothing scrolls sideways. **The scheduled twin:** the heartbeat row reads a fresh stamp even while the toggle is OFF (liveness is observable independent of the feature); with it ON, the next firing inside 8 AM–5 PM business hours claims every unclaimed request exactly as one button press would (same members, same oldest-first least-loaded pick, the `SpanishInboxAutoAssign` audit row `assigned=N; members=M`); OFF, or outside business hours, nothing is assigned and the log says why; with no members configured and the toggle ON the run is refused by name, `AUTOMATION_LAST_ERRORS` carries `SpanishAutoAssign`, and the health dot + failure digest report it until a clean run clears it. A member on approved PTO can still be handed claims — the known PTO-blind limit, shared with the button. Verified on camera: `spanish-light-wide` / `spanish-light-mobile`.

S110 | Company holidays — the morning after a holiday, "Yesterday" is the last real workday | Subsystem: Server, Client (Metrics views)
  Steps:
    - On the CDR Report workbook, confirm the `Company Holidays` tab exists (call-data-reporting's `setup()` creates it) and holds a row for the most recent company holiday (e.g. `2026-09-07` Labor Day); wait for the one-hour `cdr_holidays_v1` cache to lapse or clear it from the editor
    - As any rep, on the first workday AFTER that holiday, open Metrics → My Stats
    - Read the single-day hero label and the date it shows; open the 30-day trend and count the points across the holiday week
    - Open Manage → Coverage for the week containing the holiday, then Manage → Punctuality for the same week
    - On the dashboard side, remove the row (or park it with `Active = FALSE`) and repeat step 2 after the cache lapses
    - As a manager, on that same first workday after the holiday, read the Metrics sidebar badge's tooltip (hover) and open Manage → Admin → System; open the CDR Report row's chevron (Batch 3)
  Expected: step 3 shows "Yesterday" pointing at the last real workday BEFORE the holiday (Friday for a Monday holiday), never the holiday itself, with non-zero KPIs; the trend has no point on the holiday (it is not a workday, not a zero). Step 4 marks the holiday as a holiday in both planners with the LABEL from the tab (the legend and chips read "Company holiday", not "US holiday"), and Columbus / Veterans Day are NOT marked once the tab holds any row (the federal list is only the fallback). Step 5 restores the pre-H1 behaviour for that date — and within the hour Admin → System shows the warn finding "Company Holidays tab is empty" (or "No Company Holidays tab") naming that the US-federal list is in use; with the row back, the row's detail reads `Company Holidays: sheet · N in <year>` and the finding is an ok fact. Step 6: the badge (if any) is dated the last real workday, its tooltip says "Previous workday (date)" and names the Dashboard Standards target (never 85). A `CDR_SS_ID` the deployer cannot read degrades every surface to the federal list with a Logger line, never an error — and the CDR row's own fail finding covers it (the probes run only on a reachable store).

S111 | One answer rate, one standard — a rep and their manager see the same verdict | Subsystem: Server, Client (Metrics views, Time Clock views)
  Steps:
    - On the dashboard side, confirm the CDR Report workbook has a `Dashboard Standards` tab with a row for this team's dashboard dept (CSR: 92 / 2 on a seed install) and a `*` row; note the Team Avg Excludes names
    - As a CSR rep, open Metrics → My Stats for a day with calls; note the % Answered figure, the "target N%" chip, and the colour of the team table's % Ans cell for yourself under Team Metrics
    - As the CSR manager on the Department Dashboard, open My Department for the same day and read the same agent's Answer % and its tint
    - Back in team-tools open the Dashboard (Time Clock) card's % Answered KPI and the manager sidebar badge
    - On the dashboard side, park the team's row AND the `*` row (or rename the tab), clear this app's `cdr_standards_v1:*` cache from the editor, and reload My Stats
    - On the dashboard side, restore the row with the Amber Band cell BLANK, clear the cache, and reload My Stats, Team Metrics and the Dashboard card (Batch 3)
  Expected: steps 2 and 3 show the SAME whole-percent Answer % (both divide by answered + missed and round to the integer, as the dashboard's cell does) and the SAME tier for it — green at/above 92, amber from 90 to 91.9, red below — and the "target" chip says 92% "· from the Dashboard Standards tab", not 85%. A rep whose window has rung legs but nothing answered or missed shows a dash in the % Ans cell and the hero, never 0%. The team-average line on My Stats omits the excluded names' rows from its cohort (the benchmark), while Team Metrics' totals row still counts them. Step 4 tones % Answered with the same 2-pt amber band, and the badge's tooltip names the Dashboard Standards target. Step 5 renders NO target chip, NO tone on the % Ans cells and NO sidebar badge — never the old 85 — with the muted line "no Dashboard Standards row for this department — no target, tone or badge" on both heroes, `getMetricsAmbient` answering `unavailable: 'standard'`, and a warn finding "No answer standard for CSR" on Admin → System within the hour (an UNREADABLE tab is a different finding, "could not be read", with the reader's error). Step 6: with a target and NO band, one point under the target is RED on the team table AND the Dashboard card (no amber tier — `mtAnswerBand_`), and the CDR row's detail reads `· no amber band`.

S106 | Diagnostics retention tier — the ViewUsage / ClientErrors purge | Subsystem: Server, Client (Call Notes views)
  Steps:
    - As an admin, open Manage → Admin → Config → Retention and read the "Diagnostics tabs (PHI-free)" rows beneath the three call-note windows; open Manage → Admin → System and read the Storage inventory's ADP row
    - Re-run `installAutomationTriggers()`; with both windows at 0, run `purgeOldDiagnostics` from the editor and read the log + the AuditLog
    - Set `CLIENT_ERR_RETENTION_DAYS` to 30 (the danger confirm), leave `VIEW_USAGE_RETENTION_DAYS` at 0; hand-edit one old `ClientErrors` row's column-A timestamp to garbage; run the purge again and inspect both tabs, the `DiagnosticsPurge` audit row and Admin → System
    - Seed a `ClientErrors` tab with more than 2000 rows older than the window (or lower the window on a busy deployment) and run the purge twice
    - From an OLDER client build (or a hand-crafted `saveRetentionConfig` payload carrying only the three call-note keys), save the Retention panel
    - As a non-manager, call `google.script.run...purgeOldDiagnostics()` from the console
  Expected: both rows read "kept forever (0 = disabled)" and the ADP row's retention reads "Kept · diagnostics tabs — ViewUsage kept · ClientErrors kept". With both windows 0 the run returns BEFORE the lock, deletes nothing, writes no audit row, and the Automation Health job table carries no `DiagnosticsPurge` line (a job that legitimately writes nothing is not checked — INV-186). With the 30-day window set: `ClientErrors` rows older than 30 days are gone, `ViewUsage` is untouched, the garbage-timestamp row SURVIVES (an unparseable stamp is never deleted), the audit row reads counts only (`clientErrors=N; viewUsage=0; …`), the Automation Health job table now shows the purge's last run, and the ADP row reads "ClientErrors 30d". The over-budget run deletes 2000 rows, stamps `hitPerRunCap` in its audit row, and the second run drains the rest — never a half-deleted hole in the middle of the tab (deletes are contiguous runs from the bottom). The older client's save changes NEITHER diagnostics window (the server writes a key only when the payload carries it). The non-manager call throws "manager access required". Verified on camera: `admin-config-light-wide` (the rows) and `admin-system-light-wide` (the ADP retention text).
S107 | PTO accrual dry run — the preview predicts the credit and writes nothing | Subsystem: Server
  Steps:
    - From the Apps Script editor, select `previewPtoAccruals` and click ▶; read the execution log
    - Note one accruing rep's owed months, hours and predicted days; open the Employees sheet and note their column I balance and column R stamp
    - Run `previewPtoAccruals` a second time, then re-read both cells
    - Pick a rep the preview reports at zero and read the WHY ZERO line; cross-check it against the Timesheet (are there rows under that employee id at all? do they pair up?)
    - Run `creditMonthlyPtoAccruals`, then compare the balance delta against the preview's predicted days and the `PtoAccrualCredit` audit row against the preview's line
    - As a non-manager, call `google.script.run...previewPtoAccruals()` from the console
    - Turn `enablePtoTracking` OFF and run the preview again
    - Run `previewPtoAccruals` with a COMPLETED month the stamp has already closed (e.g. `previewPtoAccruals('2026-08')`), then re-read column I and column R
    - Pick `previewPtoAccrualsLastMonth` from the editor's function dropdown and ▶ Run it
    - Run it with `'2026-13'`, with a month NAME, and with the CURRENT month
  Expected: the log opens "PTO accrual preview — NOTHING WAS WRITTEN" and names the Timesheet range it read. Column I and column R are UNCHANGED after either preview run — the preview takes no ScriptLock and writes no cell, no balance and no audit row. A zero rep carries a WHY ZERO line naming one of three causes (no Timesheet rows at all — with the employee id it looked under; punches that never formed a complete pair; a genuine month off), and that line matches what the Timesheet shows. The credit then moves the balance by EXACTLY the previewed days and its audit row carries the same hours, rate and uncounted-day counts — the preview shares `planPtoAccrualRun_` with it, so a disagreement is a defect, not a rounding artefact. The non-manager call throws "manager access required". With the flag off the preview still reports the full plan and states "enablePtoTracking is OFF — the real job will credit NOTHING until it is on" rather than returning empty. The INSPECT run reports that month's hours for every accruing rep even though the ordinary preview says nothing is owed, opens "PTO accrual INSPECTION of <month>", states that column R was IGNORED, marks each settled rep with the rewind remedy, phrases its total as a valuation ("is WORTH") and does NOT tell the operator to run the credit — and column I and column R are unchanged afterwards. `previewPtoAccrualsLastMonth` needs no argument (the ▶ Run button passes none) and produces the SAME report as inspecting the previous completed month by name. The malformed and current-month calls are REFUSED with `success:false` and an error naming why, without reading the Timesheet at all.
S108 | PTO accrual reconciliation — late data heals, a shortfall does not claw back | Subsystem: Server
  Steps:
    - Pick an accruing rep with a COMPLETED month already credited (column R at or past it). Leave one day in that month clocked-in with no clock-out, or create one via the Timesheet
    - Run `previewPtoAccrualsLastMonth` and read the Reconcile section
    - Approve the missing punch (Manage → the rep's Day Edit, or the adjustment queue), then run `previewPtoAccrualsLastMonth` again
    - Run `creditMonthlyPtoAccruals` from the editor; re-read column I and the rep's `PtoAccrualCredit` rows in the AuditLog
    - Run `creditMonthlyPtoAccruals` a second time with nothing changed
    - Delete the clock-out you approved, then run `creditMonthlyPtoAccruals` again; re-read column I and open Manage → Admin → System
    - Hand-edit an old `PtoAccrualCredit` row's Notes cell so `hoursWorked=` is unreadable, then run the credit again
  Expected: the first preview names the open day and shows no top-up owed for it (the hours are not readable yet). After the approval the preview's Reconcile section reads "credited for N h, now reads M h → the next credit run TOPS UP D day(s)". The credit run then moves column I by exactly D and appends a SECOND `PtoAccrualCredit` row naming itself `TOP-UP +D day(s)` and carrying `hoursWorked=M` — the new total, so the next run reconciles against it. The second run credits NOTHING (idempotent on hours paid, which is what lets this pass run daily). After the deletion the balance is UNCHANGED — a shortfall is never clawed back — and Admin → System reports "was credited for M h … but the Timesheet now reads … the balance was NOT reduced; check for a deleted punch". The unreadable row FAILS CLOSED: it is skipped with a reported reason, and nothing is re-credited from a ledger that could not be read.
S109 | Open-punch prevention — the daily scan, its two bounds, and a failed scan that does not read clean | Subsystem: Server
  Steps:
    - Leave one rep with a clock-in and no clock-out THREE days ago, and one with a clock-in and no clock-out TODAY
    - Run `checkOpenPunches` from the editor and read the log; then open Manage → Admin → System
    - Wait for (or manually run) `sendAutomationHealthDigest` and read the manager email
    - Create an open day just inside `CONFIG.ADJUST_WINDOW_DAYS` (e.g. 26 days ago) and re-run the scan
    - Create an open day OLDER than the adjust window (e.g. 40 days ago) and re-run
    - Temporarily point `ADP_SS_ID` at a nonexistent id (or rename the Timesheet tab), run the scan, and re-read Admin → System
    - As a non-manager, call `google.script.run...checkOpenPunches()` and `...runDailyChecks()` from the console
    - Re-run `installAutomationTriggers` and count the Triggers panel
  Expected: the three-day-old open day is reported; TODAY's is NOT (the tail grace — a rep mid-shift is not a defect, and a rep ahead of the manager anchor must not be either). Admin → System names the rep, the day count and the window; the health email carries the same text without this job sending mail of its own. The 26-day-old day gets the extra "falls out of the N-day adjust window within a week" line; the 40-day-old day is NOT reported at all, and the panel's window text states the boundary rather than implying those days are fine. With the store broken the panel reads "the scan could not run … an empty open-punch list below is NOT a clean board" — never an empty all-clear. Both non-manager calls throw "manager access required" (the dispatcher's gate does not stand in for the job's). The Triggers panel count is UNCHANGED from before this batch — `sendCallNotesUrgentDigest` now rides `runDailyChecks` instead of owning a trigger, and its old standalone trigger is gone.

S112 | OOP pricing — the lookup, the composer price picker, and area eligibility both ways (was a second `S110`; renumbered 2026-09-17 — the blocks that say "S110 (OOP)" mean this one) | Subsystem: Server, Client (Reference views), Client (Call Notes views)
  Steps:
    - Confirm the KB spreadsheet has `OopPricing` and `LocationAcceptance` tabs, then open Manage → Admin and read the OOP pricing diagnostics
    - In Reference (landing AND the Ctrl/⌘+K drawer) look up an item that has a price, one with a BLANK price cell, and one that does not exist
    - In a Call Notes external email composer, look up an item BY PRODUCT NAME and again BY HCPCS CODE; on a multi-price item read the Insert buttons offered, Insert the WITH SHIPPING one, and read the line that lands in the message
    - Before sending, change that item's price in the spreadsheet, then Send
    - Re-insert at the new price and Send for real; read the `ExternalEmailSent` row in the AuditLog
    - Insert a price, hand-edit the number in the textarea, and Send
    - Insert a price, DELETE the whole line from the textarea, and Send
    - In Reference, run the eligibility check with a Texas address against an item whose Area Eligibility is `TX`, one that is `Open`, one that is `100 miles of <a listed warehouse>`, and one with a value nobody has taught the parser
    - Run the same check from an address ~90 miles from a warehouse, and again from ~300 miles away
    - Run it from an address IN a `city` row's city, and check what that row does to the verdicts
    - Add a `warehouse` row with no Address, and a row whose `Type` is neither warehouse nor city; re-read the diagnostics
    - Rename the `LocationAcceptance` tab aside and re-run an eligibility check on a radius item
    - Rename the `OopPricing` tab aside and repeat the composer insert
    - Temporarily rename the `Price` header to something the stem list cannot match, re-read the diagnostics, and repeat the composer insert
    - **(Batch 1, 2026-09-17)** Run the eligibility check with the ITEM box filled by product name (not code) — this used to return "No item matched"
    - **(Batch 2, 2026-09-17)** With the geocoder failing (the quota spent, or a denied key), run an eligibility check and the map block's "Find nearest"
  Expected: a product-name search and a code search BOTH find the row, and the result names the ITEM (never the HCPCS code). A multi-price item offers ONE Insert per priced column, labelled, and the inserted line names which — `Drive Scout 3 Wheel (W/ Shipping Cost) — $1,070.00 (price effective 09/16/2026)`; a component column like a bare `Shipping` offers no Insert at all. The diagnostics NAME both tabs, the NAME column and whether it was found by header or fallen back to, every pricing header and the role assigned to it, the warehouse registry with addresses, the city count, every addressless or unreadable location row BY NAME, and how every eligibility value parses — with the unreadable ones listed by item. A priced item shows its price EXACTLY as the sheet displays it (no added currency symbol, no reformatted decimals); a blank-price row says "no price on file" in warning tone and offers no Insert; a miss says so and tells the rep NOT to quote a similar item. The inserted line carries the effective date. A send after a price change is REFUSED and names both the old and the new number. The successful send's audit row carries the item and the exact price quoted, and still only the recipient's DOMAIN. A hand-edited line is REFUSED ("prices must be inserted by the picker"); a DELETED line sends normally and leaves no quote in the audit row. Every eligibility result shows TWO verdicts, labelled: `TX` reads No through insurance and Yes out of pocket from outside Texas; `Open` reads Yes both ways; the radius reads the SAME both ways — the distance does not care who is paying. The unreadable value reads "cannot tell", never "no", and quotes the cell verbatim. At ~90 miles the Yes is toned apart from a flat Yes and says the drive is longer; at ~300 the No carries no hedge. A listed delivery city shows as its own line ABOVE the items, says "reference only", and **changes no verdict** — a No stays a No. The addressless warehouse and the unreadable `Type` row appear in the diagnostics and NOT in the registry. With `LocationAcceptance` renamed away the radius item reads "cannot tell" AND the page says the tab is missing by name — never a silent UNKNOWN, and never a fallback to a city centre. With `OopPricing` renamed away the lookup errors and names the tab to create — it must NOT fall through to the KB tab or any other sheet. With the `Price` header unmatched, the diagnostics report `price` MISSING and the composer offers no Insert for anything. **(Batch 1, 2026-09-17)** The composer SEND with a picker-inserted line is accepted on the operator's real sheet (the verifier keys the same header-discovered name column the picker used — before the fix every quoted send was refused as "no longer lists"); an item-name query on the eligibility check finds the row the way the price lookup does (one scorer, name OR code); the diagnostics list unreadable eligibility values by ITEM, not by code. **(Batch 2, 2026-09-17)** With the geocoder unreachable, both surfaces say the ADDRESS SERVICE could not be reached, by status, and that the address is not the problem — "Could not find that location" is reserved for a place that does not exist (g128).

S113 | Call Notes — the Scheduled-reminders and Scratchpad modals open AND close | Subsystem: Client (Call Notes views)
  Steps:
    - On the Log view click the bell → the reminders modal opens; press Close; reopen; press Escape; reopen; click the backdrop
    - Open the Scratchpad, type a line, press Close; reopen it
  Expected: every affordance closes the modal — before Batch 1 (2026-09-17) NONE did, because both hooks cleared no node and `closeOverlay` delegates entirely to a registered hook (g130, INV-214); the Scratchpad flushes the typed line before it closes and reads Saved on the next open. Pinned by the F-02 DOM close-path sweep; the `cn-sched-modal-*` visual scenarios only OPEN it.

### Frozen Subsystems
- **DELETED in cycle 13 (batch 5) — all three frozen directories are gone from the working tree and live only in git history (last present at commit `9586b29`).** They were `call-notes/` + `call-notes-legacy/` (the superseded Workspace Add-on scaffold) and `incoming/form-generator/` (the pre-port bound Apps Script the Intake module was rewritten from) — ~3k lines across 29 files that every grep hit, every agent read, and every audit had to consciously skip, while contributing nothing: `clasp` only ever pushed `web-app/`, and no live code, test, or CI step referenced them. The Add-on path is abandoned for good (org admin policy blocks Marketplace install without ticket-driven allowlisting, the same constraint that blocks the external `?form` route); the form-generator port shipped and was settled. Provenance comments in `Code.js` / `script_intake.html` now point at git history instead of a path that no longer exists.
- **To consult them:** `git show 9586b29:incoming/form-generator/filterRecommendations.js` (or `git checkout 9586b29 -- incoming/form-generator` into a scratch worktree). Nothing needs unfreezing to read them.
- The Frozen-Subsystem MECHANISM stays documented here for future use — a subsystem that is superseded but still on disk belongs in this section so audits skip it by default.

### Deploy Command
Server: `cd web-app && clasp push -f`, then Apps Script editor → Deploy → Manage deployments → Edit current deployment → Version: **New version** → Deploy. Web app picks up the change on next page load — and since 2026-08-27 open windows notice on their own: the deploy-version beacon shows each open tab a sticky "reload to get the latest version" prompt within ~20 minutes of the New version (see the beacon Key Design Decision).
Client (shell), Client (Time Clock views), Client (Call Notes views), Client (Metrics views), Client (Intake views), Client (Reference views), Client (Training views), Client (QA views), Client (public forms): same single `clasp push -f` ships all HTML partials alongside `Code.js`; same New-version deploy step.
Test Suite: same `clasp push -f`. Tests don't ship to end users — run them from the editor with `runSmokeTests()` (safe on prod) or `runAllTests()` (writes TEST_ rows, cleans up at end). Smoke on prod, full on dev nightly; `runAllTests` on prod is the exception, not the routine. `runAllTestsPartA` / `runAllTestsPartB` split a full run across two executions when one would run too long; the summary prints the DERIVED expected count.
